# KTM 300 XC 2026: aftermarket parts

4637 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 406 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 715 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3516 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
